 We'll create a simple HTTP server that       handles POST to /run and runs the command.
 We'll do:
   from http.server import                    BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
   import json
   import subprocess
   import os
   class                                      CommandHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        token = "SECRET_TOKEN"  # We'll       generate a random one and set it
```
      def do_POST(self):
          if self.path != '/run':
              self.send_error(404)
              return
          auth_header =                       self.headers.get('X-Command-Token')
          if auth_header != self.token:
              self.send_error(401)
              return
          content_length =                    int(self.headers.get('Content-Length', 0))
          body =                              self.rfile.read(content_length)
          try:
              data = json.loads(body)
              command = data.get('command')
              if not command:
                  self.send_error(400,        "Missing command")
                  return
          except json.JSONDecodeError:
              self.send_error(400, "Invalid   JSON")
              return
          # Run the command
          try:
              # We'll run with shell=True to  allow complex commands, but be cautious.
              result = subprocess.run(command,shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True,   timeout=30)
              output = result.stdout
              error = result.stderr
              return_code = result.returncode
          except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
              output = ""
              error = "Command timed out after30 seconds"
              return_code = -1
          except Exception as e:
              output = ""
              error = str(e)
              return_code = -1
          response = {
              "output": output,
              "error": error,
              "return_code": return_code
          }
          self.send_response(200)
          self.send_header('Content-type',    'application/json')
          self.end_headers()
                                              self.wfile.write(json.dumps(response).encode())
      def log_message(self, format, *args):
          # Suppress log messages
          pass
```
   def run_server(port, token):
        server_address = ('', port)
        httpd = HTTPServer(server_address,    CommandHandler)
        CommandHandler.token = token
        print(f'Starting command server on    port {port} with token {token}')
        httpd.serve_forever()
   if name == 'main':
        import sys
        port = int(sys.argv[1]) if            len(sys.argv) > 1 else 34568
        import secrets
        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        run_server(port, token)

 We'll do:
   #!/bin/bash
   set -e
   PORT=34568
   LOG_DIR="/home/toxic/command_funnel_logs"
                                              SERVER_PID_FILE="/tmp/command_server_${PORT}.pid"
                                              FUNNEL_PID_FILE="/tmp/command_funnel_${PORT}.pid"
   FUNNEL_LOG="${LOG_DIR}/funnel_${PORT}.log"
   mkdir -p "$LOG_DIR"
Generate token
   TOKEN=$(python3 -c "import secrets;        print(secrets.token_urlsafe(32))")
Kill existing
   if [ -f "$SERVER_PID_FILE" ]; then
        kill $(cat "$SERVER_PID_FILE")        2>/dev/null || true
        rm -f "$SERVER_PID_FILE"
   fi
   if [ -f "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE" ]; then
        kill $(cat "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE")        2>/dev/null || true
        rm -f "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE"
   fi
Start the command server
   python3 /home/toxic/command_server.py      "$PORT" > "$LOG_DIR/server_${PORT}.log" 2>&1 &   echo $! > "$SERVER_PID_FILE"
   echo "Started command server on port $PORT (PID: $(cat "$SERVER_PID_FILE"))"
   echo "Token: $TOKEN"
Wait for server to start
   sleep 2
Start funnel
   tailscale funnel --bg "$PORT" >            "$FUNNEL_LOG" 2>&1 &
   echo $! > "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE"
   echo "Started funnel on port $PORT (PID:   $(cat "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE"))"
Wait for funnel to initialize
   sleep 5
Get funnel URL
   FUNNEL_URL=$(grep -o 'https://[^ ]'        "$FUNNEL_LOG" | tail -1)
   if [ -z "$FUNNEL_URL" ]; then
        FUNNEL_URL=$(tailscale funnel status  --json | grep -o 'https://[^"]' | head -1)
   fi
   if [ -z "$FUNNEL_URL" ]; then
        echo "Error: Could not determine      funnel URL" >&2
        exit 1
   fi
   echo "Funnel URL: $FUNNEL_URL"
   echo "To run a command: curl -X POST -H    'X-Command-Token: $TOKEN' -H 'Content-Type:   application/json' -d '{"command":"ls -la"}'   $FUNNEL_URL/run"
   echo "Server logs:                         $LOG_DIR/server_${PORT}.log"
   echo "Funnel logs: $FUNNEL_LOG"

 We'll create a simple HTTP server that       handles POST to /run and runs the command.
 We'll do:
   from http.server import                    BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
   import json
   import subprocess
   import os
   class                                      CommandHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        token = "SECRET_TOKEN"  # We'll       generate a random one and set it
```
      def do_POST(self):
          if self.path != '/run':
              self.send_error(404)
              return
          auth_header =                       self.headers.get('X-Command-Token')
          if auth_header != self.token:
              self.send_error(401)
              return
          content_length =                    int(self.headers.get('Content-Length', 0))
          body =                              self.rfile.read(content_length)
          try:
              data = json.loads(body)
              command = data.get('command')
              if not command:
                  self.send_error(400,        "Missing command")
                  return
          except json.JSONDecodeError:
              self.send_error(400, "Invalid   JSON")
              return
          # Run the command
          try:
              # We'll run with shell=True to  allow complex commands, but be cautious.
              result = subprocess.run(command,shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True,   timeout=30)
              output = result.stdout
              error = result.stderr
              return_code = result.returncode
          except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
              output = ""
              error = "Command timed out after30 seconds"
              return_code = -1
          except Exception as e:
              output = ""
              error = str(e)
              return_code = -1
          response = {
              "output": output,
              "error": error,
              "return_code": return_code
          }
          self.send_response(200)
          self.send_header('Content-type',    'application/json')
          self.end_headers()
                                              self.wfile.write(json.dumps(response).encode())
      def log_message(self, format, *args):
          # Suppress log messages
          pass
```
   def run_server(port, token):
        server_address = ('', port)
        httpd = HTTPServer(server_address,    CommandHandler)
        CommandHandler.token = token
        print(f'Starting command server on    port {port} with token {token}')
        httpd.serve_forever()
   if name == 'main':
        import sys
        port = int(sys.argv[1]) if            len(sys.argv) > 1 else 34568
        import secrets
        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        run_server(port, token)
 We'll then set up the funnel for that port.
 Let's create the setup script.
 However, note: The user wants it to be       detached and backgrounded.
 We'll do:
   #!/bin/bash
   set -e
   PORT=34568
   LOG_DIR="/home/toxic/command_funnel_logs"
                                              SERVER_PID_FILE="/tmp/command_server_${PORT}.pid"
                                              FUNNEL_PID_FILE="/tmp/command_funnel_${PORT}.pid"
   FUNNEL_LOG="${LOG_DIR}/funnel_${PORT}.log"
   mkdir -p "$LOG_DIR"
Generate token
   TOKEN=$(python3 -c "import secrets;        print(secrets.token_urlsafe(32))")
Kill existing
   if [ -f "$SERVER_PID_FILE" ]; then
        kill $(cat "$SERVER_PID_FILE")        2>/dev/null || true
        rm -f "$SERVER_PID_FILE"
   fi
   if [ -f "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE" ]; then
        kill $(cat "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE")        2>/dev/null || true
        rm -f "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE"
   fi
Start the command server
   python3 /home/toxic/command_server.py      "$PORT" > "$LOG_DIR/server_${PORT}.log" 2>&1 &   echo $! > "$SERVER_PID_FILE"
   echo "Started command server on port $PORT (PID: $(cat "$SERVER_PID_FILE"))"
   echo "Token: $TOKEN"
Wait for server to start
   sleep 2
Start funnel

 We'll create a simple HTTP server that       handles POST to /run and runs the command.
 We'll do:
   from http.server import                    BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
   import json
   import subprocess
   import os
   class                                      CommandHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        token = "SECRET_TOKEN"  # We'll       generate a random one and set it
```
      def do_POST(self):
          if self.path != '/run':
              self.send_error(404)
              return
          auth_header =                       self.headers.get('X-Command-Token')
          if auth_header != self.token:
              self.send_error(401)
              return
          content_length =                    int(self.headers.get('Content-Length', 0))
          body =                              self.rfile.read(content_length)
          try:
              data = json.loads(body)
              command = data.get('command')
              if not command:
                  self.send_error(400,        "Missing command")
                  return
          except json.JSONDecodeError:
              self.send_error(400, "Invalid   JSON")
              return
          # Run the command
          try:
              # We'll run with shell=True to  allow complex commands, but be cautious.
              result = subprocess.run(command,shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True,   timeout=30)
              output = result.stdout
              error = result.stderr
              return_code = result.returncode
          except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
              output = ""
              error = "Command timed out after30 seconds"
              return_code = -1
          except Exception as e:
              output = ""
              error = str(e)
              return_code = -1
          response = {
              "output": output,
              "error": error,
              "return_code": return_code
          }
          self.send_response(200)
          self.send_header('Content-type',    'application/json')
          self.end_headers()
                                              self.wfile.write(json.dumps(response).encode())
      def log_message(self, format, *args):
          # Suppress log messages
          pass
```
   def run_server(port, token):
        server_address = ('', port)
        httpd = HTTPServer(server_address,    CommandHandler)
        CommandHandler.token = token
        print(f'Starting command server on    port {port} with token {token}')
        httpd.serve_forever()
   if name == 'main':
        import sys
        port = int(sys.argv[1]) if            len(sys.argv) > 1 else 34568
        import secrets
        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        run_server(port, token)
 We'll then set up the funnel for that port.

   #!/bin/bash
   set -e
   PORT=34568
   LOG_DIR="/home/toxic/command_funnel_logs"
                                              SERVER_PID_FILE="/tmp/command_server_${PORT}.pid"
                                              FUNNEL_PID_FILE="/tmp/command_funnel_${PORT}.pid"
   FUNNEL_LOG="${LOG_DIR}/funnel_${PORT}.log"
   mkdir -p "$LOG_DIR"
Generate token
   TOKEN=$(python3 -c "import secrets;        print(secrets.token_urlsafe(32))")
Kill existing
   if [ -f "$SERVER_PID_FILE" ]; then
        kill $(cat "$SERVER_PID_FILE")        2>/dev/null || true
        rm -f "$SERVER_PID_FILE"
   fi
   if [ -f "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE" ]; then
        kill $(cat "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE")        2>/dev/null || true
        rm -f "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE"
   fi
Start the command server
   python3 /home/toxic/command_server.py      "$PORT" > "$LOG_DIR/server_${PORT}.log" 2>&1 &   echo $! > "$SERVER_PID_FILE"
   echo "Started command server on port $PORT (PID: $(cat "$SERVER_PID_FILE"))"
   echo "Token: $TOKEN"
Wait for server to start
   sleep 2
Start funnel
   tailscale funnel --bg "$PORT" >            "$FUNNEL_LOG" 2>&1 &
   echo $! > "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE"
   echo "Started funnel on port $PORT (PID:   $(cat "$FUNNEL_PID_FILE"))"
Wait for funnel to initialize
   sleep 5
Get funnel URL
   FUNNEL_URL=$(grep -o 'https://[^ ]'        "$FUNNEL_LOG" | tail -1)
   if [ -z "$FUNNEL_URL" ]; then
        FUNNEL_URL=$(tailscale funnel status  --json | grep -o 'https://[^"]' | head -1)
   fi
   if [ -z "$FUNNEL_URL" ]; then
        echo "Error: Could not determine      funnel URL" >&2
        exit 1

   echo "Funnel URL: $FUNNEL_URL"

   echo "Server logs:                         $LOG_DIR/server_${PORT}.log"
   echo "Funnel logs: $FUNNEL_LOG"

    - Expose at path "/files"
2. For the command server:
    - Listen on localhost:34568 (HTTP)
    - Expose at path "/cmd"

   tailscale serve --bg --set-path /cmd 34568
But note: the serve command expects the port  first? Actually, from the help, the syntax is:
   tailscale serve [flags] <target>
And the flags include --set-path. So we can   do:
   tailscale serve --bg --set-path /files     34567
